# Funding tracker — Dr. Yi Ru / AXIOMALITY

Generated 2026-09-25 from `opportunities.json` (443 entries). Tiers: A = prepare now; B = this cycle after securing a sponsor/nominator or 6–15 months out; C = monitor; D = ineligible / not worth it. Deadline confidence: H/M/L. Verify every date on the official page before relying on it.
